- **session-advisor**: Use this agent when the user wants a review of their open Claude sessions. Typical triggers include the /manager command, "which sessions are stale", "what is waiting on me", and "what can I archive". It reads session metadata and recent messages, sorts each session, and returns a short to-do list. It never changes a session. ([jay-stacey/claude-plugins](https://github.com/jay-stacey/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/session-manager/agents/session-advisor.md))

- **session-auditor**: Session-history analyst — indexes session logs and extracts turns, tool-call patterns, recurring work, and lessons; use for workflow profiling and post-incident audits ([nurmdrafi/pi-native](https://github.com/nurmdrafi/pi-native/blob/HEAD/agents/session-auditor.md) · ★4)
- **session-auditor**: USE WHEN: 用户调 `/session-review` 想看当前 session 工作流执行情况(flow 推进 / agent 边界 / blocker 处理)。OUTPUT: 审查报告(只读) 或 flow 配置修复(`--fix`)。DO NOT USE: 修业务代码(只改 flow 配置) / 跨 session 趋势审查(走 workflow-reviewer) / 单纯查 task 状态(直接读 task.json)。 ([dd-xiaozhi/dev-agent-flow](https://github.com/dd-xiaozhi/dev-agent-flow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **session-auditor**: Audits a Claude Code session transcript for token spend and harness-usage quality — wasted calls, missed delegation, cache misses — and writes a graded retrospective. Use after a session ends. ([prototypdigital/bluetemberg-packs](https://github.com/prototypdigital/bluetemberg-packs/blob/HEAD/packages/bluetemberg-agents-session-auditor/llm/agents/session-auditor.md) · ★1 · 11 copies)
- **session-auditor**: Read-only Pi session audit specialist for recent-session reviews, workflow friction analysis, tool-error metrics, cost and token analysis, and evidence-backed extension, skill, agent, or instruction improvements ([adaminglehart/pi-config](https://github.com/adaminglehart/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Orchestrates one Claude Code session's audit end to end -- runs the cost and timeline extractors, fans out to 5 general-purpose shards (4 parallel, 1 sequential), merges their digests, and renders the self-contained audit_session-&lt;sid&gt;.html. ([brunogsa/unix-utils](https://github.com/brunogsa/unix-utils/blob/HEAD/configs/ai-docs/claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Use after a work session (or when compact thrashing / context churn is suspected) to audit session transcript files for token waste, redundant reads, missed vault recalls, and compaction causes. Produces a waste report with concrete memory/skill fixes. ([davidbillera-lab/personal-OS](https://github.com/davidbillera-lab/personal-OS/blob/HEAD/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Reviews what changed in a session and checks against CLAUDE.md rules ([Khaledaun/Yalla-london](https://github.com/Khaledaun/Yalla-london/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Session audit critic; dispatched with ROLE=bug_hunt or ROLE=claim_bust. Read-only adversarial lens on invoking-thread deliverables. Returns structured findings only — no writes, no fixes. ([mrkinglollipop/manna-links](https://github.com/mrkinglollipop/manna-links/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor**: Audit Pi SDK session storage for unbounded growth, missing TTLs, and cross-tenant data leaks. ([srbryers/Yumi](https://github.com/srbryers/Yumi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-auditor.md))
- **session-auditor-agent**: Process auditor that maintains a living timeline of the review session. Use to audit what was done, by which agent, when, and what it produced — tracking gaps and decisions. ([sixfeetup/2026_AllThingsAI_OrchestrateAgenticAI](https://github.com/sixfeetup/2026_AllThingsAI_OrchestrateAgenticAI/blob/HEAD/cowork-plugin/agents/session-auditor-agent.md) · ★10)

- **session-bridge**: Cross-session context persistence — auto-saves decisions, conventions, progress, instincts to .rune/ files. Loads + integrity-checks at session start. Auto-triggered before compaction. ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/session-bridge.md) · ★93)
- **session-bridge-agent**: Use when a subagent should save or load cross-session state in .topia/ (decisions, conventions, progress), for example before compaction or at session start. Runs the session-bridge skill's steps. ([linenoize/topia](https://github.com/linenoize/topia/blob/HEAD/agents/session-bridge-agent.md) · ★1)
- **session-brief**: What every agent session on this project needs to know. A session prompt names only the issue and what is specific to it; the rest is here. Shared rules live in the workflow kit and are linked, not copied. ([DcBexter/MetaboliteDetector](https://github.com/DcBexter/MetaboliteDetector/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/session-brief.md))
- **session-brief**: Reads process docs, architecture docs, active plans, and the last commit to produce a concise session brief. Run at the start of any session to get up to speed without re-exploring the codebase. ([SoapyD/3D_Map_Generator](https://github.com/SoapyD/3D_Map_Generator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/_session-brief.md))
- **session-brief-agent**: Haiku-powered context cache producing a 24h-cached per-account session brief. Consumed by orchestrator and workflow skills' Phase 0 context loading to reduce redundant per-day reads. Plugin-scoped, never invoked directly by user. ([migos1990/presales-harness-starter](https://github.com/migos1990/presales-harness-starter/blob/HEAD/agents/session-brief-agent.md) · ★2)
- **session-briefer**: Comprehensive session briefings for instant context understanding. Triggers on "get up to speed with @sessions/[SESSION_NAME]" or "brief me on @sessions/[SESSION_NAME]". ([elevanaltd/eav-cep-assist](https://github.com/elevanaltd/eav-cep-assist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-briefer.oct.md) · 2 copies)
- **session-briefer**: Briefeur de session CYNA — lit le graphe mémoire au démarrage et produit un briefing complet : état actuel, tâches en attente, risques actifs, patterns à surveiller. Invoquer en PREMIER à chaque nouvelle session de travail. ([Valdrin72/gestion-chantier](https://github.com/Valdrin72/gestion-chantier/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-briefer.md))
- **session-buddy-specialist**: Use proactively for anything touching the Session-Buddy MCP server — its 49 tools, the Phase 1/1.5 signer feed, the Phase 3 agent catalog, the reflection database, and the lifespan-owned ``SkillsSigner``. Routes through ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_list_skills`` and ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_get_skill`` for skill discovery and through ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_list_agents`` plus ``mcp__session_buddy__session_buddy_get_agent`` for agent installs. Self-published from the session-buddy server (genuinely new — no adjacent specialist in the ecosystem; see Scope). ([lesleslie/session-buddy](https://github.com/lesleslie/session-buddy/blob/HEAD/session_buddy/mcp/agents/session-buddy-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **session-budget**: Session length is governed by CONTEXT SIZE, never by a task count. Re-reading a long context is the orchestrator's largest cost, and a session that fills mid-task is the hardest one to stop. So plan the work so that you are always one **clean logical break** away from stopping: all work committed, the tracker current, no sub-agent running. ([Zenosyne-Technologies/agent-operating-kit](https://github.com/Zenosyne-Technologies/agent-operating-kit/blob/HEAD/templates/marvin/agents/session-budget.md))
- **session-check**: Run the read-only repository check before implementation: ([LaunchAstro/ops-astro](https://github.com/LaunchAstro/ops-astro/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/session-check.md) · 2 copies)
- **session-checkpoint**: Use this agent when context usage exceeds approximately 50% capacity and you need to prepare for a `/clear` to continue work in a fresh session. Also use this agent when a significant milestone is reached mid-session and you want to capture progress before moving on to the next phase. This agent reads the current plan, completed work, and remaining tasks to produce a structured checkpoint file — it never modifies source code.\n\nExamples:\n\n- Example 1:\n Context: The user has been working on implementing F5 shortlist feature and context is getting long.\n user: "Let's continue implementing… ([milos-agathon/buurt-check](https://github.com/milos-agathon/buurt-check/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/session-checkpoint.md) · ★4)
- **session-classifier**: Classifies Claude Code session digests into fixed facets for the sessions-to-fluency skill. Use only when that skill hands over a batch of digests. ([Mesh-AI/agentic-engineering-fluency-plugin](https://github.com/Mesh-AI/agentic-engineering-fluency-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-engineering-fluency/agents/session-classifier.md))
- **session-close**: Run when the user says "wrap up". Writes an achievement tracking entry to the project's Obsidian vault. Encodes corrections as CLAUDE.md rules and memory entries. Creates any new agents or skills identified during the session and pushes them to the scaffold repo. Takes a structured brief from the invoking Claude instance. Does not write session summaries or update workstream notes - those are maintained during the session. ([cjeggo/claude-code](https://github.com/cjeggo/claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/session-close.md))
- **session-close-background-work-handover-2026-10-03**: Research synthesis, 2026-10-03. Question: how should a multi-session Claude Code setup (a coordinator, a watcher or a fan-out lane) close a session without losing its background work, and can a successor session take that work over? Invoked by the ramp-up-session skill or a supervising coordinator so a main session can delegate the context-expensive reading to a sub-agent -- the dormant session's transcript never floods the caller's context. Not user-invocable directly. The agent is personality-neutral: it returns a compact, factual situation report and next-actions, never raw transcript dumps and never a persona of its own. Consulted before any non-trivial implementation starts. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-architect.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-architect**: SwarmFusion architect. Use proactively for system design, task decomposition, and planning before any non-trivial implementation. Produces ordered, independently verifiable plans. ([andrewr303/claude-codex-plugin-lab](https://github.com/andrewr303/claude-codex-plugin-lab/blob/HEAD/plugins/swarmfusion/agents/sf-architect.md))
- **sf-architect**: Design stage of the Foreman pipeline for Salesforce projects. Reads one story, the project's metadata and the slot org's schema, decides whether it can be built now, picks the Salesforce builder and writes the design and test plan the builder follows. Does not write files. ([Finefactory-ai/foreman](https://github.com/Finefactory-ai/foreman/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/sf-architect.md))
- **sf-architect**: Use when cross-module contracts, boundaries or system structure need a decision. Owns architecture and interface contracts; uses BMad architecture skills when installed. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-architect.md))
- **sf-architect**: ユーザーが「要件定義書を作りたい」「機能設計書を生成して」「設計書を作って」「組織分析して」「ユーザーストーリーを作って」「影響調査して」と言ったとき。Salesforceの要件定義・設計書作成・組織解析・影響調査・ユーザーストーリー作成に特化。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-architect.md))
- **sf-architecture**: You are the **Org Architecture** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. Your job is to assess the org's technical architecture — custom object and field sprawl, governor limit consumption, Apex code quality, installed packages, and the use of modern vs. legacy configuration patterns. ([iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude](https://github.com/iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-architecture.md) · ★4)
- **sf-architecture-reviewer**: Independent Architecture Reviewer of the Software Factory cell. Use after sf-code-reviewer has cleared every module of a feature, as the second and final serial review stage before security scanning and merge. Reviews the INTEGRATED result across all parallel modules — hexagonal boundary violations, contract drift, cross-module duplication/inconsistency the parallel developer agents couldn't see in each other, and undocumented technical debt. The one gate specifically responsible for catching what a per-module review structurally cannot. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-architecture-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-aura-reviewer**: Use when reviewing or maintaining Aura components for architecture, events, Locker/LWS compliance, performance, and LWC migration readiness. Do NOT use for LWC components or Apex-only review. ([jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code](https://github.com/jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-aura-reviewer.md) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **sf-automation**: You are the **Automation Health** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. Your job is to inventory all automation in the org, identify legacy technologies that need migration, detect conflicts and anti-patterns, and assess overall automation hygiene. ([iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude](https://github.com/iaesteman/ai-salesforce-audit-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-automation.md) · ★4)
- **sf-backfill**: Syncs Salesforce ARR and contract end dates into Notion Active Packages — fills null ARRs, corrects stale end dates, handles renewal rollovers (deactivate old + create new), and surfaces churn/skip cases in chat for review. Never auto-updates churned or at-risk accounts. ([kmtz3/aise-leadership](https://github.com/kmtz3/aise-leadership/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-backfill.md))
- **sf-benchmark-fidelity**: Audits ONE ScreamingFace Benchmark against its references (the paper, the authors' original harness and, for an Imported Benchmark, inspect_evals + inspect_ai at the pinned versions) and returns a Fidelity Record body with one verdict: comparable-to-paper, comparable-to-inspect, not-comparable or unverified. Read-only on the repo; runs pinned third-party code only in a throwaway environment with no keys; never calls a model. Invoke with a catalogue id (`ifeval`, `inspect-gsm8k`) and, for the planted-deviation calibration only, an our-side override path. ([ScreamingFace/screamingface](https://github.com/ScreamingFace/screamingface/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-benchmark-fidelity.md) · ★24)
- **sf-bugfix-agent**: Diagnose and fix Salesforce build errors, Apex test failures, metadata conflicts, and deployment issues with minimal diffs. Use PROACTIVELY when builds or deploys fail. Do NOT use for new features or refactoring. ([jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code](https://github.com/jiten-singh-shahi/salesforce-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/sf-bugfix-agent.md) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **sf-build**: SaaSFoundry build tier: claude-sonnet-5-5 at xhigh effort. Use for multi-file-feature (a feature spread over several files), tests (writing or repairing tests). Do not use for exploration, prose (use sf-light); scoped-edit (use sf-edit); bug-fix, review (use sf-fix); planning (use sf-plan); terminal-ci (use sf-terminal). ([DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI](https://github.com/DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-build.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sf-builder**: Execution agent. Checks consumers before changing. Builds and verifies per task. Follows existing patterns exactly. ([shipfast-ai/shipfast](https://github.com/shipfast-ai/shipfast/blob/HEAD/agents/builder.md) · ★8)
- **sf-builder**: Builds an APPROVED /sf-ticket spec in a per-ticket git worktree and deploys it to the build sandbox (dry-run first, new automation inactive). QA-org writes prompt the operator; Prod is blocked. Use only after the case folder has approved.json. Writes build.md. ([beck-systems/salesforce-agentic-sdlc](https://github.com/beck-systems/salesforce-agentic-sdlc/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-builder.md) · ★1)
- **sf-builder**: SangeetFit implementation specialist. Use when a single clearly bounded implementation task needs doing inside an assigned file-ownership scope — smallest complete change, directly relevant tests, honest reporting. Not for read-only investigation or whole-feature design. ([Pr1meGG/SangeetFit](https://github.com/Pr1meGG/SangeetFit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-builder.md))
- **sf-code-review**: ScreamingFace-specific code reviewer. Reviews a branch, PR, worktree, or diff it did not write — read-only, reports findings, never fixes. Encodes this repo's mined bug history (as of Sept 2026: ~400 work ledgers, ~90 reviewed PRs, the architecture docs) as seven review lanes with concrete file anchors, plus the context recipe, severity triage, and noise list that make findings trustworthy. Invoke with an explicit target (branch / PR number / worktree path / file list) and, when known, the OME-N ticket. ([ScreamingFace/screamingface](https://github.com/ScreamingFace/screamingface/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-code-review.md) · ★24)
- **sf-code-reviewer**: Independent Code Reviewer of the Software Factory cell. Use after a module's implementation and its own tests are green, BEFORE it proceeds to architecture review. Reviews the diff for correctness, contract adherence, Clean Code, and — critically — whether the tests actually prove what they claim to, since the agent who wrote the code and its tests under TDD is structurally blind to its own blind spots. Never the same agent that implemented or tested the module under review. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-code-reviewer**: Review stage of the Foreman pipeline for Salesforce projects. Runs beside compliance-reviewer and security-reviewer once the tester approves; reviews Apex, LWC, Flow, metadata, OmniStudio and Agent Script against Salesforce practice and runs Code Analyzer. Reports; does not fix. ([Finefactory-ai/foreman](https://github.com/Finefactory-ai/foreman/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md))
- **sf-code-reviewer**: Reviews Apex, triggers, and LWC against GForce standards — fflib layering, DataAccess.USER_MODE, bulkification, governor limits, NebulaLogger, 90% coverage. TRIGGER when: reviewing a diff or file containing .cls, .trigger, or LWC changes. DO NOT TRIGGER when: the change is TypeScript, Terraform, workflow YAML, or Dockerfiles, even in a repo that also contains Apex. ([Gforce-Innovation-Kft/sf-develop-demo](https://github.com/Gforce-Innovation-Kft/sf-develop-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-code-reviewer**: Use for independent review of the actual diff against contracts, principles and evidence, with cited anchors. Reviews; never authors the work under review. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-code-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-codebase-scanner**: Analyzes codebase for concerns, quality issues, and improvement opportunities. Writes SCAN.md directly. ([ivkan/specflow-cc](https://github.com/ivkan/specflow-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/codebase-scanner.md) · ★1)
- **sf-context-librarian**: Select the smallest SfSkills skill/reference set for an observed Salesforce deployment failure. Returns paths, reasons, and estimated context cost. Does not diagnose. ([PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills/blob/HEAD/integrations/cursor/agents/sf-context-librarian.md) · ★26)
- **sf-context-loader**: SFプロジェクトの docs/ からタスク内容に関連するコンテキストのみを選択的に抽出して要約するエージェント。通常モード（docs/ 全体を構造化マッチングでスキャン）と knowledge-only モード（backlog.md〔investigator の分〕・backlog-planner から focus_hints:["knowledge-only"] で呼ばれ case-index/pitfalls/sf-standard/decisions の4ファイル限定 Grep）の2モードをサポート。backlog系・release-preparer・sf-architect・assistant 等から Phase 0 として呼ばれる。無関係なタスクや docs/ 未整備プロジェクトには「該当コンテキストなし」を返す。knowledge-only モードが参照するのは docs/knowledge/ のキュレーション済みナレッジ文書であり、Backlog の実課題データ・docs/logs/ 対応実績ログという一次情報の参照は pattern-curator が担当する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-context-loader.md))
- **sf-coverage**: You are the **Test Coverage** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. - **sf-critic**: Review agent. Multi-depth code review — quick for small changes, deep for complex. Traces imports and data flow. ([shipfast-ai/shipfast](https://github.com/shipfast-ai/shipfast/blob/HEAD/agents/critic.md) · ★8)
- **sf-critic**: 완성된 원고 전체(또는 큰 덩어리)를 문예 비평가의 시선으로 평가한다. 구조, 인물 아크, 주제의 정합성, SF 아이디어가 인간의 이야기로 작동하는지를 본다. ([Ssojux2/novel-wiki](https://github.com/Ssojux2/novel-wiki/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-critic.md))
- **sf-data-quality**: You are the **Data Quality** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. - **sf-debugger**: SpecForge 调试 Agent，负责分析和修复 executor 执行失败的技术问题 ([lyqstart/SpecForge](https://github.com/lyqstart/SpecForge/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/2026-11-08-crossplatform-consumer-audit/original-documents/setup/userlevel-opencode/agents/sf-debugger.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-deploy-engineer**: Runs wave-3 Salesforce deployments - validate first, then quick-deploy the recorded job id. Use for manifest and destructive-change handling, test-level selection, and rollback planning. Refuses production without a validated job and explicit human approval. ([grzmol/vibe-force](https://github.com/grzmol/vibe-force/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-deploy-engineer.md) · ★3)
- **sf-descriptions**: You are the **Description Completeness** subagent for a Salesforce org audit. - **sf-design**: SpecForge 设计 Agent，负责系统治理分析、架构演进、接口定义、数据模型和验证策略 ([lyqstart/SpecForge](https://github.com/lyqstart/SpecForge/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/2026-11-08-crossplatform-consumer-audit/original-documents/setup/userlevel-opencode/agents/sf-design.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-design-reviewer**: Use when a plan or diff needs an anti-bloat design review. Applies the four sf- design skills: reuse first, the smallest complete diff, no unrequested abstraction. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-design-reviewer.md))
- **sf-design-step1**: sf-design コマンドから委譲される詳細設計ステップ専用エージェント。グループ確定・詳細設計の実行・必要に応じた後続エージェント（sf-design-step2/step3）への連鎖呼び出しを担う。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-design-step1.md))

- **sf-design-step3**: sf-design コマンドから委譲される機能一覧ステップ。generate_feature_list.py を直接呼び出して機能一覧.xlsx を生成する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-design-step3.md))
- **sf-design-writer**: プログラム設計書（Excel）と機能一覧（Excel）を生成する専門エージェント。sf-design-step2 エージェントから委譲されて実行する。force-app/ と docs/ を徹底的に読み込み、高品質な設計内容 JSON を生成してから Python スクリプトで Excel に変換する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-design-writer.md))
- **sf-detail-design-writer**: 詳細設計書（Excel）を業務グループ単位で生成する専門エージェント。feature_groups.yml が示すグループ構成とソースコードを読み込み、エンジニア向けの詳細設計 JSON を生成してから Python スクリプトで Excel に変換する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-detail-design-writer.md))
- **sf-developer**: Developer of the Software Factory cell. Use to implement a feature or fix strictly via TDD (RED-GREEN-REFACTOR) against an approved spec, inside the hexagonal architecture boundaries set by the Architect. Default implementer for general (non-ML) application code. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-developer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-developer**: Build stage of the Foreman pipeline for Salesforce projects. Builds one story with Apex or Lightning Web Components, plus the metadata it needs, from the architect's design, tests first, and fixes the findings when the tester or a reviewer sends it back. ([Finefactory-ai/foreman](https://github.com/Finefactory-ai/foreman/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/sf-developer.md))
- **sf-devils-advocate**: SwarmFusion devil's advocate. Use after a conclusion, plan, or unanimous approval to attack the consensus and surface what everyone missed. ([andrewr303/claude-codex-plugin-lab](https://github.com/andrewr303/claude-codex-plugin-lab/blob/HEAD/plugins/swarmfusion/agents/sf-devils-advocate.md))
- **sf-devops-engineer**: DevOps Engineer of the Software Factory cell. Use to build CI/CD pipelines, infrastructure as code, blue-green/canary deployments, and to manage GitHub releases and repos via gh. Owns the path from a merged PR to running production traffic. ([Kemquiros/anvil](https://github.com/Kemquiros/anvil/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-devops-engineer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **sf-discusser**: Conducts interactive discussions to clarify requirements and resolve ambiguities ([ivkan/specflow-cc](https://github.com/ivkan/specflow-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/discusser.md) · ★1)
- **sf-doc-objects-writer**: sf-doc コマンドから委譲されてオブジェクト項目定義書を生成する。Salesforce組織に直接接続して xlsx を 01_基本設計/ に出力し、両方選択時は sf-doc-overview-writer を連鎖呼び出しする。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-doc-objects-writer.md))
- **sf-doc-overview-writer**: sf-doc コマンドから委譲されるプロジェクト概要書生成エージェント。docs/ 配下の情報源を元に generate_basic_doc.py を呼び出してプロジェクト概要書.xlsx を生成する。単独実行（概要書のみ選択）または両方選択時の sf-doc-objects-writer からの連鎖呼び出しで起動する。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-doc-overview-writer.md))
- **sf-docs**: Answers a question from official Salesforce docs (developer, architect, help, admin and lightningdesignsystem.com) with verbatim quotes the script copies from the cached page, each tied to its official URL and line. Use it proactively whenever the user wants something read, scanned, checked or looked up in Salesforce documentation, even without naming this agent ("what does Help say about X", "check the Apex guide for Y", "scan the docs on Z"), and when you do not know which page holds the answer. Brief it with the question, the site or product if known, and the width ("the one concept page"… ([drsaavedra/sf-docs-agent](https://github.com/drsaavedra/sf-docs-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-docs.md) · ★4)
- **sf-driver**: Drives SentinelFlow WC3 instances for the repetitive, context-heavy work — booting instances, screenshotting, recapturing/verifying anchors, walking phase flows, and live-testing the behaviour driver. Use this whenever a task means many screenshots, crop/verify iterations, or multi-step instance navigation, so that churn stays out of the main context. Returns a terse summary (outcome + diagnostics), never frame/log dumps. Spawn with model sonnet (default); pass haiku for trivial mechanical runs. ([coller95/sentinelflow](https://github.com/coller95/sentinelflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-driver.md))
- **sf-edit**: SaaSFoundry edit tier: claude-haiku-5-5 at high effort. Use for scoped-edit (a small change confined to one or two files). Do not use for exploration, prose (use sf-light); bug-fix, review (use sf-fix); multi-file-feature, tests (use sf-build); planning (use sf-plan); terminal-ci (use sf-terminal). ([DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI](https://github.com/DiamondForgeFr/SaasFoundryAI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-edit.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sf-effort-estimator**: 工数見積専用エージェント。`/backlog` フェーズ内外を問わず「工数計算 / 工数見積 / 工数 / effort / 見積 / 何時間」系の問い全てを担当する。作業の型（作業の大きさで分けた10種）が同じ過去の課題を case-index.md と Backlog から選び、Backlog の実績時間で確かめた値の中央値から `{N}h` + 根拠 + 信頼度を返す（似た課題が足りなければ global-calibration.md の対応する種別の中央値）。頼まれたときだけ、合計を変えずにお客様向けの内訳に配分する。直接呼び出し可。 ([sodechcorp/claudecode-template](https://github.com/sodechcorp/claudecode-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sf-effort-estimator.md))
- **sf-engineer**: Use for standard implementation work with tests. Builds the smallest complete change, including error and recovery paths and obsolete-path removal, proven with the project's own commands. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/tests/goldens/cursor/agents/sf-engineer.md) · 2 copies)
- **sf-evaluator**: Use for calibrated qualitative evaluation. Runs known-good and known-bad canaries first, checks deterministic effects before judging, then grades rubrics from redacted evidence. ([sbeeredd04/software-factory](https://github.com/sbeeredd04/software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/sf-evaluator.md))
- **sf-evidence-collector**: SpecForge 证据收集 Agent，负责在验证阶段收集和组织执行证据（命令输出、测试结果、文件变更记录） ([lyqstart/SpecForge](https://github.com/lyqstart/SpecForge/blob/HEAD/setup/userlevel-opencode/agents/sf-evidence-collector.md))
- **sf-evidence-reviewer**: Independently reject unsupported claims, missing citations, contradictions, unsafe Salesforce mutations, overconfidence, and undeclared unknowns in a deployment triage draft. - **sf-executor**: SpecForge 执行 Agent，负责执行单个已通过 Gate 的 task，修改指定文件并报告结果 ([lyqstart/SpecForge](https://github.com/lyqstart/SpecForge/blob/HEAD/.patch-backups/r5_20260615_123529/repo/setup/userlevel-opencode/agents/sf-executor.md) · 4 copies)

- **sf-field-sprawl**: You are the **Custom Field Sprawl** subagent for a Salesforce org audit.
